The above subgroups H of G gives G/H-coverings X/H->P2 of P2 isomorphic to the 
same T-covering of P2, where T=<T.1,..,T.3> is the group Z2^3. The building data
of the T-covering Y->P2 are the following:
[
<T.1 + T.2 + T.3, 1>,
<T.2 + T.3, 1>,
<T.2 + T.3, 1>,

<T.3, 1>,
<T.1, 1>,
<T.1, 1>
]

of the T-covering Y->P2 are the following:
[

<T.3 + T.4 + T.5 + T.6, 1>,
<T.1, 1>,
<T.2, 1>,
<T.3, 1>,
<T.4, 1>,
<T.5, 1>,
<T.6, 1>
]
